## Filtered synthesis adopted as system doctrine (~11:01 EET)
- [decision] The full studied library (22 Instagram links, hooks library, both sheets, PDFs, Founder-Led book, Fraser videos, competitor studies, owner taste rules) was filtered into adopt/reject rulings that now stand as doctrine. Full file saved at `intelligence/zyra-synthesis-filtered-2026-11-20.md` and summarized in ~/MEMORY.md.
- ADOPTED: LadyTabtab's trust machine WITHOUT their discount engine (licensing, 6–12-month formula testing, real named reviews; Zyra's 34 Drive reviews to be decomposed into personas + verbatim headline lines); Nadera's calm premium (full ingredients on PDP, no discount engine — offers page empty) + the finding that the KP field is open (their Haririya scrub carries no KP claim); Flair-style founder-voiced EGC (real sell-outs, next-day restock, behind-the-scenes) from launch day 22 Nov in Ahmed's voice; the Founder-Led protocol (one 4-hour shoot day ≈ 34 pieces; cohorts = post-weight-loss/loose-skin, new mom, bride-to-be; 30-question prompt → customer question map); Ibrahim's scale ladder from 1 Dec (Target CPA = AOV ÷ ROAS; ATC gate 12%, IC gate 45%; SCALE +20–40% / PUSH / HOLD 24h / OPTIMIZE / PULL BACK) on Zyra numbers (COGS 350, target CPP 150–160) — before Dec, no ad judged before 48h AND 2× target CPP spend; Zyra hooks bank in Egyptian built on customer wounds; comment-gated educational carousels (keyword comment → DM link); retargeting after week 1 (hot = WhatsApp engagers, warm = 75%+ viewers, cold = broad); new bundles priced via the profitability simulator before fixing; Nadera-style product pages with shipping/returns policy visible.
- REJECTED (standing): discount engines/coupons, playful English product names, AI-generated product shots / AI ad generation (real white-bg packshots only; take the method, not the generator), literal translation of foreign reels (translate the principle/format, never the words), any claim without mechanism + percentage, an ad that tries to say everything (one purpose per ad), scaling by feel.
- NADRH ruling: the only direct KP competitor (urea 30% + glycolic, WhatsApp-only, no website) — monitor, never respond; beat them with the real website, trust, and bundles, not a price war.
- Machine gaps logged as Muse's work orders: (1) the visual gate is now formal — taste rules, visual mass judged first, zero-defect QC, freeze broken only by Ahmed's word; (2) competitor creative reading must deepen for the key competitors (the 4-hour rotation sees each of 33 only every ~5.5 days — too slow for creative moves); (3) Meta manual intake is a known fragility (API blocked; human uploads 2×/week). Decisions keep arriving NUMBERED for his «اعتمد/ارفض + رقم» replies.
- [fact] Ahmed's exams are 23–24 November 2026. Launch is Sun 22 Nov. Standing consequence: ALL heavy launch decisions must be closed BEFORE 23 Nov; during 23–24 the fleet monitors and only true emergencies reach him.

## TWO-GATE PROTOCOL — owner order (~11:05 EET)
- [decision] Standing order, supersedes lenient verdict habits: every item Ahmed or the designer sends passes a VISUAL gate and a COPY gate BEFORE any reply, scored /100 each with the breakdown stated honestly (protocol file: `creative/gate-protocol-2026-11-20.md`). Visual gate runs FIRST (half-second test 20, one eye-stopping moment 15, one scene not slabs 15, products-as-heroes 15, realism 10, type weight 10, eye path/intentional space 10, native-size composition 5). Copy gate: locked-copy match 25, plain-Egyptian proofread incl. banned words 25, claims-with-mechanism+percentage + retinol 20, segment-tailored CTA 15, verified unified Latin numerals 15. Bands: 90+ ship; 80–89 ship after named micro-fix; 65–79 numbered fix list, no ship; <65 rejected/rebuild. The piece must pass BOTH; the lower score governs.
- Calibration anchors: approved full-bleed stories 85–89; KA-1 story v5 = visual 53 (owner's rejection matched); KA-1 1x1 = visual 86 / copy 100. Ahmed explicitly missed the old scored verdicts («كنت بتعملي أرقام وتقييم زي 80 و90 وبتقيم صح») — scored, honest evaluation is the required reply format for all design/content reviews from now on.

## PDP audit — KP routine page, read as a customer not a developer (~11:13 EET)
- [event] Ahmed asked Muse to check the store's product pages and name the weakest thing + how to develop it for highest CR («وشيكلي على صفحات المنتج عندي فلستور وقولي اي اصعف حاجه وازاي نطورها لاعلى cr»), then corrected the stance: «كانك عميل مش مطور» — audit as a customer arriving from an ad on mobile. Muse read the live homepage, products.json, and the full KP routine PDP (zyra-care.com/products/kp-calm-smooth-routine) as that customer.
- [fact] WEAKEST THING named: the KP PDP teaches well (what it solves / why it happens / common mistakes / steps / results-over-time) but does not reassure at the decision moment — the «من رسايل عميلاتنا» section contains not one readable review with a name and a specific result. The customer's barrier is trust, not price (zero price objections in the 34 studied chats); her question "will it work on me?" is unanswered in a peer's voice. Nourhan's «تقريبًا ٨٠٪ جلد الوزة اختفى» exists in the Drive data but is NOT on the page. LadyTabtab (the trust machine) puts verbatim reviews at the heart of its pages.
- [fact] Further PDP findings, in order: (1) the offer never appears inside the journey — ~240 lines of persuasion with no «799 بدل 1,050 + الدفع عند الاستلام» and no buy button mid-page; the customer must scroll back up; (2) the free-shipping gap is invisible on-page — no «ضيفي بـ 201 ج والشحن علينا» and no add-on recommendation, so the 998-basket trap starts here; (3) gallery is only 2 images for a 799 EGP decision (packshot + usage graphic) — no texture, no per-product shots, no size-in-hand; (4) only active ingredients are listed, not the full INCI list (Nadera shows full ingredients — the premium standard); (5) fine-comb hits on the live page: banned «ليّن» is an official step name («2. نليّن») and «بتقفل على الترطيب» repeats 3×; three numeral systems coexist on one page («٢٥٪» / «25٪» / «799.00»); the shea is named «الخام المكرره» in the steps — a self-contradiction.
- [decision] CR development plan delivered, in priority order: (1) real proof block — 3–4 reviews with names, verbatim customer wording and usage duration, placed right after «النتيجة مع الوقت», plus one proof line under the buy box (written consents per §7 rule); (2) offer card inside the journey after the steps and after the timeline (799 vs 1,050, «وفّرتي 251», COD) + sticky mobile buy bar; (3) basket completion on the page itself — «كمّلي 1,000 والشحن علينا» recommending the Splash 240 (makes 1,039), NOT the mukhmaria (makes 998); (4) gallery to 5–6 images (real texture, each product alone, size in hand); (5) voice/numeral clean-up — remove ليّن/تقفل from the whole page, one numeral system, fix the shea name; (6) full ingredient list per product in an accordion, for the checking segment that recommends to others.
- [fact] Honesty caveat recorded with the audit: the read was from the page's content and order; the sticky bar's actual appearance on mobile still needs an eye on the live browser for visual confirmation.

## Smart upsell design — delivered, awaiting approval (~11:28 EET)
- [event] Ahmed asked for a smart upsell customized to him («عايز اب سيل ذكي مخصص ليا») to maximize AOV. Muse delivered a 5-layer design built on Zyra's own numbers/behavior, all offers computed on the post-discount basket total: (1) Threshold engine — live «ناقصك X» bar + ONE recommendation chosen as the smallest add that crosses 1,000 without a trap: 799 routine-only basket → Splash 240 (1,039) or Shea 250 (1,049), mukhmaria 199 BANNED here (makes 998); if 100OFF stays (basket 699) no single add crosses, so the engine offers a pair (mukhmaria + splash same scent = 1,138) or the second routine — one more reason the code decision must close pre-launch; recommendation tinted by problem/season (winter → shea first, scent buyer → splash). (2) Routine completion on single-product pages: lotion-only buyer (360) sees «كمّلي الروتين بـ 439 بدل 690 ووفري 251» — sells a result, not a product. (3) Duration upsell: 2 routines = the full 8-week course (matches the announced 3–8-week result window) at 1,598 + free shipping, saving framed as shipping/continuity, NO product discount (consistent with the anti-discount-engine stance). (4) Confirmation-call upsell — one tailored add in the existing pre-shipping confirmation call script (winter KP → extra shea for elbows/heels; scent buyer → same-scent mukhmaria), refusable before shipping so delivery rate is protected. (5) Second-problem cross: dual hero 1,598 + free shipping.
- [fact] Guardrails stated: never auto-add anything, one offer per moment, and every layer measured on confirmation + delivery rates, not AOV alone (a bigger refused basket is a loss). Scenario estimate (labeled as such): current AOV 779 → ~880–920 if the threshold engine converts 3/10 routine-only baskets to 1,039 and duration takes 1/10, with zero new customers. Phasing: layers 1+3+4 at launch, then 2+5. Muse offered to write the full developer spec (all basket states) if Ahmed approves — approval pending.

## Brief 1 (Mechanism Journey D11) execution v1 — REJECTED, rebuild in a real scene (~11:52–11:55 EET)
- [event] Execution arrived with supplied scores (story 93, feed 91). Muse verified sizes from the files (story 1080×1920, feed 1080×1080) and scored independently: story visual 83 / copy 100, feed 78 / copy 100 — defects: shea MISSING (only 2 of the 3 routine products shown), products too small to be heroes (~12% of feed width), realism weak (only a thin real-texture strip; rest gradient illustration).
- [correction — owner, supersedes Muse's 83/78] Ahmed caught 3 deeper flaws Muse missed: (1) the abstract skin cross-section does not read as skin in half a second — layers read as sand/cake, the dotted channel reads as a staircase, not a pore; a mechanism needing decoding fails the half-second test, and decoding is the most expensive thing in an ad; (2) the brown «التراكم» cluster reads as gravel/chocolate, potentially dirt — disgust risk; (3) the arrow + «التراكم» label are infographic devices breaking Muse's own standing "ad, not infographic" rule (already written in visual-gate item 3), and real products pasted into an abstract drawing read as fake composites.
- [state] Muse revised the verdict down: story 70 / feed 66 — visual rebuild, copy stays locked at 100. Rebuild direction recorded in the brief file and in `creative/gate-protocol-2026-11-20.md` as a standing calibration: abandon the abstract cross-section; the mechanism is told in a REAL scene — real magnified skin texture with clear pores, droplets entering one hero pore, no arrows/labels/diagram devices; all 3 products as real packshots in a real setting. D11 survives only as real macro photography, never an abstract render.
